- **explainer**: Explain how a piece of code works and teach where the answer lives. Use when the user runs /explainer, or asks a code question such as "where does X get set", "how do we fetch Y", "what script does Z", "how does this flow work", or "why does this happen". The answer traces the real call chain, cites the file and line for every claim, shows the code that proves it, shows how to find it again, and, when the code is on a branch or MR that is not checked out, opens a worktree so the user can read it. Do not use it to summarize an MR or PR (use read-mr) or a ticket or thread (use read-issue).
